# pcm-only: files 55501–55800 of 57745

[Catalogue index](index.md). [Method and limits](../hvsc-rom-requirements.md).

K-only removes BASIC; B-only removes KERNAL. All rows include duration, evidence class, reference RMS/write count, and the three removal outcomes. `derived` marks proven zero-read equivalence, not a separate render.

## Synth Music VI - Kraftwerk

Path: `MUSICIANS/W/Winterberg_Michael/Synth_Music_VI-Kraftwerk.sid`

Author: Michael Winterberg · Release/year: 1986 Michael Winterberg

PSID; BASIC flag clear; 1 subtunes. Complete-file MD5: `3f9bdd7d5d09d0023678126f897f9bd0`; SHA-256: `4b414d3368a915e468ef6bdf5f5f092b68c14a6de893052391bc9298b72fcd7a`.

| Subtune | Seconds | Evidence | Reference RMS; writes | Neither | K-only | B-only |
|---:|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2 | pcm-difference-with-similar-write-sequence | 5.889e-2; 1291 | ΔRMS 5.488e-2; writes 1291; prefix 1291; drift 56; derived | identical PCM; writes 1291; prefix 1291; drift 0; derived | ΔRMS 5.488e-2; writes 1291; prefix 1291; drift 56 |

STIL (full available entry and applicable directory notes):

> Directory note /MUSICIANS/W/Winterberg_Michael/
> COMMENT: "It's a long time ago that I composed sounds for the good old C64. But
>          all I remember is that it was a very great time with much fun.
>          My composing started in the years 1983/84 when I programmed my own
>          music-editor and the music-play-routine. I made an extra-routine for
>          each sound. From music to music I changed that routine to get
>          different sounds. I have made some musics, good and bad sounds. But I
>          have tried to make people happy with listening to my music."
>          (Comment by Winterberg himself.)
>
> TITLE: Neon Lights [from The Man-Machine] (0:00)
>  ARTIST: Kraftwerk
>   TITLE: The Model [from The Man-Machine] (0:33)
>  ARTIST: Kraftwerk
>   TITLE: Showroom Dummies [from Trans Europe Express] (1:21)
>  ARTIST: Kraftwerk
